# Hebrew word list (binding; append with `>>`, never rewrite)

One Hebrew (or Aramaic) word → one English and one Czech rendering wherever the
sense is the same. Check the word is not already here before adding a row. A
row binds where the sense is the same; where it would say something wrong in a
verse, keep the verse right and say why in Changes.

## Standing decisions

- Infinitive absolute + verb: "surely" + verb (מוֹת תָּמוּת "you shall surely
  die"; CZ „jistě zemřeš“), literal form once per chapter in Changes.
- Oath formula אִם / אִם לֹא: by meaning ("I will not …" / "I will surely …"),
  literal in Changes.
- אַף of anger: "anger" ("his anger burned"; CZ „vzplanul jeho hněv“); "nose /
  nostrils" where the body is meant; note the word once per chapter.
- בֵּן / בְּנֵי: "son(s) of" (also "sons of Israel", "son of man"); not "children",
  "people", "descendants" unless the Hebrew has another word.
- אִישׁ "man", אָדָם "man" / "mankind" (token `adam` as the lexicon has it), אֱנוֹשׁ
  "mortal man", גֶּבֶר "mighty man": keep them apart where they stand together.
- Supplied words follow WORDS.md "Standing decisions" (Greek list): the same
  rules hold here.
